<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="507" data-end="653"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JavaScript<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>if (!progresso) {

<h2 data-section-id="auh3re" data-start="760" data-end="787">Estrutura final do while</h2>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="789" data-end="975"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JavaScript<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>while (...) {

  let progresso = false

  for (...) {

    ...

    if (podeExecutar) {
      ...
      progresso = true
    }

  }

  if (!progresso) {
    erro
  }

}

<p data-start="0" data-end="49">Passo 81: Corrigir a posição do <code data-start="32" data-end="49">if (!progresso)```</p>
<p data-start="51" data-end="103">Está <strong data-start="56" data-end="71">quase certo</strong>, mas tem um detalhe importante:</p>
<p data-start="105" data-end="115">👉 O bloco</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="117" data-end="160"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JavaScript<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>if (!progresso) {
  ...
}


<p data-start="162" data-end="260">ficou <strong data-start="168" data-end="187">dentro do <code data-start="180" data-end="185">for```</strong>, e ele precisa ficar <strong data-start="209" data-end="226">fora do <code data-start="219" data-end="224">for```</strong>, mas ainda <strong data-start="238" data-end="259">dentro do <code data-start="250" data-end="257">while```</strong>.</p>
<p data-start="262" data-end="310">Substitua esse trecho inteiro pelo bloco abaixo:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="312" data-end="887"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JavaScript<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>while (ordemDasTarefas.length < body.tarefas.length) {

  let progresso = false

  for (const tarefa of body.tarefas) {

    if (tarefasResolvidas.has(tarefa.nome)) {
      continue
    }

    const podeExecutar = tarefa.dependencias.every(dep => tarefasResolvidas.has(dep))

    if (podeExecutar) {
      ordemDasTarefas.push(tarefa.nome)
      tarefasResolvidas.add(tarefa.nome)
      progresso = true
    }
  }

  if (!progresso) {
    return sendJson(res, 400, {
      sucesso: false,
      erro: "Dependências inválidas ou ciclo detectado"
    })
  }
}

    erro: "Payload inválido: cada tarefa precisa ter 'tempo'"
  })
}

const { otimista, provavel, pessimista } = tarefa.tempo

if (
  typeof otimista !== "number" ||
  typeof provavel !== "number" ||
  typeof pessimista !== "number"
) {
  return sendJson(res, 400, {
    sucesso: false,
    erro: "Payload inválido: tempos devem ser números"
  })
}

if (otimista > provavel || provavel > pessimista) {
  return sendJson(res, 400, {
    sucesso: false,
    erro: "Payload inválido: ordem dos tempos deve ser otimista <= provavel <= pessimista"
  })
}

}

<p data-start="168" data-end="185">O erro está aqui:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="187" data-end="256"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JavaScript<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>const podeExecutar = tarefa.dependencias.every(...)

<h2 data-section-id="9vdcvs" data-start="519" data-end="555">✅ Correção (simples e definitiva)</h2>
<p data-start="557" data-end="623">Dentro do <strong data-start="567" data-end="584">for principal</strong>, antes de usar <code data-start="600" data-end="608">.every```, garante isso:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="625" data-end="714"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JavaScript<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>if (!Array.isArray(tarefa.dependencias)) {

<h2 data-section-id="1c7tymu" data-start="799" data-end="820">🧠 Analogia rápida</h2>

> 
<p data-start="924" data-end="957">se não existir, vira lista vazia.</p>

<hr data-start="682" data-end="685">
<h2 data-section-id="8hmkdd" data-start="687" data-end="712">🧩 Substitui por isso:</h2>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="714" data-end="1042"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JavaScript<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>for (const tarefa of body.tarefas) {

  if (tarefasResolvidas.has(tarefa.nome)) {
    continue
  }

  // 🔥 GARANTE QUE EXISTE ARRAY
  const dependencias = Array.isArray(tarefa.dependencias)
    ? tarefa.dependencias
    : []

  const podeExecutar = dependencias.every(dep =>
    tarefasResolvidas.has(dep)
  )

# ChatGPT

<p data-start="0" data-end="43">Tá certo — só tem um detalhe do PowerShell.</p>
<p data-start="45" data-end="156">👉 Quando dá erro 400, o <code data-start="70" data-end="89">Invoke-WebRequest``` <strong data-start="90" data-end="127">não mostra o body automaticamente</strong> (mesmo com <code data-start="139" data-end="154">Select-Object```).</p>
<hr data-start="158" data-end="161">
<h2 data-section-id="del85x" data-start="163" data-end="208">✅ Como ver a mensagem de erro corretamente</h2>
<p data-start="210" data-end="221">Roda assim:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="223" data-end="720"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>PowerShell<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>try {
  Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "http://localhost:3000/executar-projeto" `
    -Method POST `
    -Headers @{ "Content-Type" = "application/json" } `
    -Body '{ "projeto": { "nome": "Teste" }, "tarefas": [ { "nome": "Tarefa 1", "fase": "Planejamento", "tipo": "estrategica", "dependencias": [], "tempo": { "otimista": 5, "provavel": 2, "pessimista": 4 } } ] }'
} catch {
  $_.Exception.Response.GetResponseStream() |
  % { (New-Object System.IO.StreamReader($_)).ReadToEnd() }
}


<hr data-start="722" data-end="725">
<h2 data-section-id="1magaez" data-start="727" data-end="752">🎯 O que você deve ver</h2>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="754" data-end="880"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JSON<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>{
  "sucesso": false,
  "erro": "Payload inválido: ordem dos tempos deve ser otimista <= provavel <= pessimista"
}

<p data-start="0" data-end="33">Passo 96: Entender a fórmula PERT</p>
<p data-start="35" data-end="89">Agora vamos começar o cálculo real do tempo da tarefa.</p>
<p data-start="91" data-end="108">A fórmula é esta:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="110" data-end="171"><button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>PERT = (otimista + 4 * provavel + pessimista) / 6


<p data-start="173" data-end="184">👉 Exemplo:</p>

<ul data-start="185" data-end="231">
<li data-section-id="pusaz8" data-start="185" data-end="199">
<p data-start="187" data-end="199">otimista = 1</p>

<li data-section-id="40od36" data-start="200" data-end="214">
<p data-start="202" data-end="214">provável = 2</p>

<li data-section-id="1huz3hb" data-start="215" data-end="231">
<p data-start="217" data-end="231">pessimista = 4</p>


<p data-start="233" data-end="243">Resultado:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="244" data-end="289"><button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>(1 + 4*2 + 4) / 6 = 13 / 6 = 2.17


<p data-start="291" data-end="399">Por enquanto, não mexe no código. Só guarda a ideia: vamos transformar os 3 tempos em <strong data-start="377" data-end="398">1 tempo calculado</strong>.</p>
<p data-start="401" data-end="433" data-is-last-node="" data-is-only-node="">Você entendeu a fórmula do PERT?</p>

<hr data-start="474" data-end="477">
<h2 data-section-id="1gtxmtn" data-start="479" data-end="500">👉 Troca por isso:</h2>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="502" data-end="1028"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JavaScript<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>if (podeExecutar) {

  const dependencias = Array.isArray(tarefa.dependencias)
    ? tarefa.dependencias
    : []

  let maiorTempoDependencias = 0

  for (const dep of dependencias) {
    const tempoDep = duracaoAcumulada[dep] || 0
    if (tempoDep > maiorTempoDependencias) {
      maiorTempoDependencias = tempoDep
    }
  }

  duracaoAcumulada[tarefa.nome] =
    maiorTempoDependencias + tarefa.tempoCalculado

  ordemDasTarefas.push(tarefa.nome)
  tarefasResolvidas.add(tarefa.nome)
  progresso = true
}

<h2 data-section-id="1bmn7ta" data-start="356" data-end="376">🧠 O que isso faz</h2>
<p data-start="378" data-end="442">Se a tarefa atual crítica depende da tarefa que estamos olhando:</p>

<h2 data-section-id="l8ta3c" data-start="566" data-end="587">📍 Estrutura agora</h2>
<p data-start="589" data-end="615">Seu <code data-start="593" data-end="598">for``` vai ficar assim:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="617" data-end="1154"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JavaScript<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>for (let i = ordemDasTarefas.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
  const nomeTarefa = ordemDasTarefas[i]
  const tarefa = tarefasPorNome[nomeTarefa]
  const tempoAcumulado = duracaoAcumulada[nomeTarefa]

  if (tempoAcumulado === tempoTotalProjeto &amp;&amp; !tarefaCriticaAtual) {
    caminhoCritico.push(nomeTarefa)
    tarefaCriticaAtual = tarefa
    continue
  }

  if (
    tarefaCriticaAtual &amp;&amp;
    tarefaCriticaAtual.dependencias.includes(nomeTarefa)
  ) {
    caminhoCritico.push(nomeTarefa)
    tarefaCriticaAtual = tarefa
  }
}

# User

const TIPOS_VALIDOS = ["estrategica", "operacional", "tecnica"]

for (const tarefa of body.tarefas) {

  // nome
  if (!tarefa.nome || typeof tarefa.nome !== "string") {
    return sendJson(res, 400, {
      sucesso: false,
      erro: "Payload inválido: cada tarefa precisa ter 'nome'"
    })
  }

  // fase
  if (!tarefa.fase || typeof tarefa.fase !== "string") {
    return sendJson(res, 400, {
      sucesso: false,
      erro: "Payload inválido: cada tarefa precisa ter 'fase'"
    })
  }

  const faseNormalizada = tarefa.fase
  .normalize("NFD")
  .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
  .replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, "")
  .toLowerCase()

  const mapaFases = {
   planejamento: "Planejamento",
   programacao: "Programacao",
   execucao: "Execucao",
   controle: "Controle",
   execuo: "Execucao"
  }

 tarefa.fase = mapaFases[faseNormalizada] || tarefa.fase

  // tipo
  if (!tarefa.tipo || !TIPOS_VALIDOS.includes(tarefa.tipo)) {
    return sendJson(res, 400, {
      sucesso: false,
      erro: "Payload inválido: 'tipo' deve ser estrategica, operacional ou tecnica"
    })
  }

  // tempo
  if (!tarefa.tempo || typeof tarefa.tempo !== "object") {
    return sendJson(res, 400, {
      sucesso: false,
      erro: "Payload inválido: cada tarefa precisa ter 'tempo'"
    })
  }

  const { otimista, provavel, pessimista } = tarefa.tempo

  if (
    typeof otimista !== "number" ||
    typeof provavel !== "number" ||
    typeof pessimista !== "number"
  ) {
    return sendJson(res, 400, {
      sucesso: false,
      erro: "Payload inválido: tempos devem ser números"
    })
  }

  if (otimista > provavel || provavel > pessimista) {
    return sendJson(res, 400, {
      sucesso: false,
      erro: "Payload inválido: ordem dos tempos deve ser otimista <= provavel <= pessimista"
    })
  }

  const pert = (otimista + 4 * provavel + pessimista) / 6
  tarefa.tempoCalculado = pert

}

<p data-start="43" data-end="120">Agora vamos preparar a ferramenta que vai calcular datas reais no cronograma.</p>
<p data-start="122" data-end="196">👉 Antes do <code data-start="134" data-end="173">const server = http.createServer(...)```, adicione esta função:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="198" data-end="367"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JavaScript<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>function adicionarDias(data, dias) {
  const novaData = new Date(data)
  novaData.setDate(novaData.getDate() + Math.ceil(dias) - 1)
  return novaData
}


<p data-start="369" data-end="513">👉 Essa função pega uma data inicial e soma a duração da tarefa.

Estamos usando <code data-start="451" data-end="468">Math.ceil(dias)``` para arredondar o tempo calculado para cima.</p>

<p data-start="299" data-end="320">adicione este trecho:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="322" data-end="881"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JavaScript<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>let dataInicioTarefa = new Date(dataInicioProjeto)

      maiorFim = fimDep
    }
  }

  dataInicioTarefa = new Date(maiorFim)

}

<p data-start="0" data-end="41">Passo 135: Estruturar saída para o Notion</p>

<p data-start="234" data-end="262">Antes do <code data-start="243" data-end="251">return```, adicione:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="264" data-end="555"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JavaScript<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>const tarefasFormatadas = body.tarefas.map(t => ({

  fim: t.cronograma.fim,

  critica: caminhoCritico.includes(t.nome)
}))

<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="254" data-end="294"><button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>abc123def4567890abc123def4567890

<ul data-start="306" data-end="397">

<h2 data-section-id="mu28la" data-start="404" data-end="435">⚠️ Atenção (pegadinha comum)</h2>

<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="475" data-end="519"><button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>abc123de-f456-7890-abcd-1234567890ef

<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="627" data-end="694"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>env<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>NOTION_DATABASE_TAREFAS=abc123def4567890abc123def4567890


<hr data-start="696" data-end="699">
<h2 data-section-id="yqrga1" data-start="701" data-end="747">🔥 Passo importante que muita gente esquece</h2>

<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="354" data-end="403"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>JavaScript<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>http.createServer(async (req, res) => {

<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="515" data-end="959"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#e45ab3" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>PowerShell<button class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copiar" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-df3050c8.svg#ce3544" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button>Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "http://localhost:3000/executar-projeto" `

}' | Select-Object -ExpandProperty Content
